```python
"""Search for low-score nonnegative step-function heights.

The score is scale-invariant.  All optimization is performed on normalized
nonnegative sequences.  Several complementary solvers are used:

1. L-BFGS-B on a softmax reparameterization, minimizing a smooth
   log-sumexp of the squared-convolution coefficients.
2. A projected / multiplicative smoothed-max gradient loop.
3. Line-search directions from linearizations:
   - the stated-of-the-art LP direction  max sum(g) s.t. f*g <= M,
   - a tangent LP on the first-order approximation of the active
     convolution peaks.
4. Random block / low-pass initializations and resampled versions of the
   provided construction.

The final answer is selected from the best internally found candidates and,
if available, re-validated by the official evaluate_sequence routine.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize, linprog
except Exception:
    minimize = None
    linprog = None


_CTX = None
_STOP_DEADLINE = 0.0


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Basic utilities
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def normalize_nonneg(a):
    """Return a 1-D float array with nonnegative entries summing to 1."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        a = np.ones_like(a, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(a.size)
    return a / s


def softmax_from_log(w):
    """Stable softmax."""
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    w = w - float(np.max(w))
    e = np.exp(w)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        e = np.ones_like(w, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(e.size)
    return e / s


def fft_conv(a):
    """Linear convolution a*a using FFT.  Assumes a is 1-D."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    size = 1
    while size < m:
        size <<= 1
    fa = np.zeros(size, dtype=np.float64)
    fa[:n] = a
    fa = np.fft.rfft(fa)
    out = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, size)
    out = out[:m]
    out[out < 0.0] = 0.0
    return out


def fft_cross_conv(a, b):
    """Linear convolution a*b using FFT.  a and b are 1-D."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = a.size
    m = b.size
    total = n + m - 1
    if total <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    size = 1
    while size < total:
        size <<= 1
    fa = np.zeros(size, dtype=np.float64)
    fb = np.zeros(size, dtype=np.float64)
    fa[:n] = a
    fb[:m] = b
    out = np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(fa) * np.fft.rfft(fb), size)
    out = out[:total]
    out[out < 0.0] = 0.0
    return out


def cross_pow(p, a):
    """
    Return r[j] = sum_i a[i] * p[i+j].

    p has length 2n-1 when it is a convolution vector.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = a.size
    m = p.size
    if n == 0 or m == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    need = n + m - 1
    size = 1
    while size < need:
        size <<= 1
    fa = np.zeros(size, dtype=np.float64)
    fb = np.zeros(size, dtype=np.float64)
    fa[:n] = a
    fb[:m] = p[::-1]
    out = np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(fa) * np.fft.rfft(fb), size)
    idx = (m - 1) - np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
    r = out[idx]
    r[~np.isfinite(r)] = 0.0
    r[r < 0.0] = 0.0
    return r


def score_positive(a):
    """Score of a normalized nonnegative sequence using FFT convolution."""
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    c = fft_conv(a)
    M = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(M):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * M)


def score_exact_limit(a, max_n=4096):
    """Use exact np.convolve for moderate sizes; otherwise FFT."""
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    n = a.size
    if n <= max_n:
        s = float(np.sum(a))
        if s <= 1e-300:
            return float("inf")
        c = np.convolve(a, a)
        M = float(np.max(c))
        if not np.isfinite(M):
            return float("inf")
        return float(2.0 * n * M / (s * s))
    return score_positive(a)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Search state helpers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def update_global(state, seq, score):
    """Record a new candidate if it improves the running best."""
    if state is None:
        return False
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return False
    seq = normalize_nonneg(seq)
    if seq.size == 0:
        return False

    old = float(state.get("score", float("inf")))
    if score >= old - 1e-12:
        return False

    state["score"] = float(score)
    state["seq"] = seq.copy()

    top = state.get("top")
    if top is None:
        top = []
        state["top"] = top

    if len(top) < 12:
        top.append((float(score), seq.copy()))
    else:
        worst_idx = 0
        worst_val = top[0][0]
        for i in range(1, len(top)):
            if top[i][0] > worst_val:
                worst_val = top[i][0]
                worst_idx = i
        if score < worst_val:
            top[worst_idx] = (float(score), seq.copy())

    if not np.isfinite(old) or score < old - 1e-5:
        print(f"new internal best: score={score:.12f} n={seq.size}", flush=True)
    return True


def load_provided_sequence():
    """Load height_sequence_1 if present, normalized and nonnegative."""
    try:
        raw = height_sequence_1  # global supplied by the environment
    except Exception:
        return None
    try:
        a = np.asarray(raw, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    except Exception:
        return None
    if a is None or a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    if not np.isfinite(float(np.sum(a))) or float(np.sum(a)) <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return normalize_nonneg(a)


def resize_to_length(a, N):
    """Resize a sequence to length N by linear upsampling or bin-sum downsampling."""
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    N = int(max(1, N))
    if a.size == N:
        return a
    if a.size < N:
        return upsample(a, N)
    return downsample(a, N)


def downsample(a, N):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    N = int(max(1, N))
    m = a.size
    if N == 1:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if m <= N:
        return upsample(a, N)
    out = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
    for i in range(N):
        lo = (i * m) // N
        hi = ((i + 1) * m) // N
        if hi > lo:
            out[i] = float(np.sum(a[lo:hi]))
    return normalize_nonneg(out)


def upsample(a, N):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    N = int(max(1, N))
    m = a.size
    if N == 1:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if m == N:
        return a
    if m == 1:
        return np.ones(N, dtype=np.float64) / float(N)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, float(m - 1), N)
    i0 = np.floor(x).astype(np.int64)
    i1 = np.minimum(i0 + 1, m - 1)
    frac = x - i0
    out = a[i0] * (1.0 - frac) + a[i1] * frac
    return normalize_nonneg(np.maximum(out, 0.0))


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Initializers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def make_initial(N, kind, rng, best_seq=None, provided_seq=None):
    """Create a normalized nonnegative starting sequence of length N."""
    N = int(max(2, N))

    if kind == -1 and best_seq is not None:
        return resize_to_length(best_seq, N)
    if kind == -2 and provided_seq is not None:
        return resize_to_length(provided_seq, N)
    if kind in (-1, -2):
        kind = 5

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, N)

    if kind == 0:
        mu = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 0.8))
        sig = float(rng.uniform(0.10, 0.38))
        a = np.exp(-0.5 * ((x - mu) / sig) ** 2)

    elif kind == 1:
        alpha = float(rng.choice([-3.0, -2.0, -1.0, 1.0, 2.0, 3.0]))
        a = np.exp(alpha * x)

    elif kind == 2:
        alpha = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.5))
        beta = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.5))
        xs = np.linspace(1.0 / (N + 1), float(N) / (N + 1), N)
        a = xs ** alpha * (1.0 - xs) ** beta

    elif kind == 3:
        L = int(rng.integers(2, max(3, N // 2)))
        p = float(rng.uniform(0.10, 0.90))
        a = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
        if L >= N:
            L = N - 1
        a[:L] = p / L
        a[L:] = (1.0 - p) / max(1, N - L)

    elif kind == 4:
        K = int(rng.integers(2, 7))
        edges = np.sort(rng.random(max(1, K - 1)))
        breaks = np.concatenate(([0.0], edges, [1.0]))
        lengths_float = np.diff(breaks)
        lengths = np.maximum(1, (N * lengths_float).astype(np.int64))
        diff = N - int(np.sum(lengths))
        while diff > 0:
            lengths[int(rng.integers(K))] += 1
            diff -= 1
        while diff < 0:
            idx = int(np.argmax(lengths))
            if lengths[idx] > 1:
                lengths[idx] -= 1
                diff += 1
            else:
                break
        masses = rng.dirichlet(np.ones(max(1, K)))
        a = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
        pos = 0
        for L, mass in zip(lengths, masses):
            L = int(L)
            if L > 0 and pos + L <= N:
                a[pos:pos + L] = float(mass) / L
                pos += L
        if pos < N:
            a[pos:] = 0.0

    elif kind == 5:
        base = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=N)
        wlen = max(1, min(N, 1 + max(1, N // 25)))
        if wlen > 1:
            w = np.ones(wlen, dtype=np.float64)
            w /= w.sum()
            base = np.convolve(base, w, mode="same")
        a = np.exp(0.60 * base)

    elif kind == 6:
        b = float(rng.uniform(1.0, 5.0))
        a = np.exp(-b * x)

    elif kind == 7:
        # Deterministic-ish three block skew.
        L1 = max(2, int(round(0.20 * N)))
        L2 = max(2, int(round(0.45 * N)))
        L3 = N - L1 - L2
        if L3 < 1:
            L2 = max(1, N - L1 - 1)
            L3 = 1
        masses = np.array([0.28, 0.42, 0.30], dtype=np.float64)
        masses = normalize_nonneg(masses)
        a = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
        a[:L1] = masses[0] / L1
        a[L1:L1 + L2] = masses[1] / L2
        a[L1 + L2:] = masses[2] / L3

    else:
        a = rng.random(N)

    return normalize_nonneg(np.maximum(a, 0.0))


def random_perturb(a, rng, sigma=0.10):
    """Multiplicative global and block perturbation, keeping nonnegativity."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1).copy()
    n = a.size
    if n <= 1:
        return normalize_nonneg(a)

    base = np.maximum(a, 1e-15)
    base *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, float(sigma), size=n))

    w1 = max(1, n // 250)
    w2 = max(w1 + 1, n // 10)
    width = int(rng.integers(w1, w2 + 1))
    start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width + 1)))
    base[start:start + width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 1.5 * float(sigma), size=width))

    return normalize_nonneg(base)


def add_two_block_candidates(N, state):
    """Quick two-rectangle search for a good basin."""
    N = int(max(2, N))
    length_fracs = np.linspace(0.12, 0.48, 10)
    mass_vals = np.linspace(0.10, 0.90, 12)
    for lf in length_fracs:
        L = int(round(lf * N))
        L = int(min(max(2, L), N - 2))
        for p in mass_vals:
            a = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
            a[:L] = p / L
            a[L:] = (1.0 - p) / (N - L)
            a = normalize_nonneg(a)
            update_global(state, a, score_positive(a))


def add_random_block_candidates(N, state, rng, count=60):
    """A handful of random block constant starts."""
    N = int(max(2, N))
    for _ in range(int(count)):
        a = make_initial(N, 4, rng)
        update_global(state, a, score_positive(a))


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Smoothed-max projected/multiplicative steps
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def smooth_step(a, c, M, temp_frac, cur_score):
    """
    Smoothed max subgradient step.
    Returns an improved sequence or None.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    M = float(M)
    if M <= 1e-300:
        return None

    T = max(1e-15, float(temp_frac) * M)
    s = (c - M) / T
    s = np.clip(s, -60.0, 0.0)
    p = np.exp(s)
    ps = float(np.sum(p))
    if not np.isfinite(ps) or ps <= 1e-300:
        p = np.zeros_like(c)
        p[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        p /= ps

    r = cross_pow(p, a)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(r)):
        r = np.where(np.isfinite(r), r, 0.0)

    best_a = a
    best_cur = float(cur_score)

    # Additive projected simplex step with backtracking.
    d = r - float(np.mean(r))
    scale = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
    if scale > 1e-300:
        alpha = 1.0
        for _ in range(12):
            h = normalize_nonneg(a - alpha * scale * d)
            sc = score_positive(h)
            if sc < best_cur - 1e-15:
                best_a = h
                best_cur = sc
                break
            alpha *= 0.5

    # Multiplicative / natural gradient-like step on a fixed positive simplex.
    dn = a * (r - float(np.dot(a, r)))
    scale_n = float(np.max(np.abs(dn)))
    if scale_n > 1e-300:
        alpha = 1.0
        for _ in range(12):
            h = normalize_nonneg(a * np.exp(-alpha * scale_n * dn))
            sc = score_positive(h)
            if sc < best_cur - 1e-15:
                best_a = h
                best_cur = sc
                break
            alpha *= 0.5

    if best_cur < cur_score - 1e-15:
        return best_a
    return None


def line_search_mix(a, g, cur_score):
    """Try convex combinations of a and normalized g."""
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    g = normalize_nonneg(g)
    best_a = a
    best_cur = float(cur_score)
    for t in (0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.35, 0.55, 0.80, 1.0):
        h = normalize_nonneg((1.0 - float(t)) * a + float(t) * g)
        sc = score_positive(h)
        if sc < best_cur - 1e-15:
            best_a = h
            best_cur = sc
    return best_a, best_cur


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# LP directions
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def choose_active_rows(c, max_rows):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    m = c.size
    if m <= 1:
        return np.arange(m, dtype=np.int64)
    max_rows = int(max(1, min(max_rows, m)))
    if max_rows >= m:
        return np.arange(m, dtype=np.int64)
    return np.argpartition(c, -max_rows)[-max_rows:].astype(np.int64)


def build_active_matrix(f, rows, scale=1.0):
    """Matrix of linear convolution operators on active lags."""
    f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = f.size
    rows = np.asarray(rows, dtype=np.int64).reshape(-1)
    A = np.zeros((rows.size, n), dtype=np.float64)
    if rows.size == 0 or n == 0:
        return A
    for r, k in enumerate(rows):
        k = int(k)
        lo = max(0, k - n + 1)
        hi = min(n, k + 1)
        if hi > lo:
            j = lo + np.arange(hi - lo, dtype=np.int64)
            A[r, j] = scale * f[k - j]
    return A


def solve_lp_sot(f, M, rows):
    """
    State-of-the-art LP direction:
        maximize sum(g)  subject to f*g <= M, g >= 0.
    Returns a normalized direction g if sum(g) > 1, else None.
    """
    if linprog is None:
        return None
    f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = f.size
    M = float(M)
    if n < 2 or M <= 1e-300:
        return None
    if rows is None:
        rows = choose_active_rows(fft_conv(f), max(64, n // 2))

    A = build_active_matrix(f, rows, 1.0)
    if A.size == 0 or not np.any(A):
        return None

    A = A / M
    try:
        res = linprog(
            -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64),
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=np.ones(A.shape[0], dtype=np.float64),
            bounds=(0.0, None),
            method="highs",
            options={"presolve": True, "max_iters": 50000},
        )
    except Exception:
        return None

    if res is not None and res.status == 0 and res.x is not None:
        g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        g = np.clip(g, 0.0, None)
        S = float(np.sum(g))
        if S > 1.0 + 1e-7:
            return normalize_nonneg(g)
    return None


def solve_lp_tangent(a, c, M, rows=None, max_rows=250):
    """
    Tangent LP direction.

    For current a and max convolution M, solve
        minimize  gamma
        subject to  L_k(g) = 2*(a*g)_k - c_k <= gamma   for active k
                   sum(g) = 1, g >= 0,
    and return g if the first-order objective gamma is below the current max.
    """
    if linprog is None:
        return None
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = a.size
    M = float(M)
    if n < 2 or M <= 1e-300:
        return None

    if rows is None:
        rows = choose_active_rows(c, max_rows)

    current_rows = np.asarray(rows, dtype=np.int64).copy()
    best_g = None
    best_gamma_abs = float("inf")

    for _ in range(3):
        A = build_active_matrix(a, current_rows, 2.0 / M)
        R = A.shape[0]
        if R == 0:
            break

        c_obj = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
        c_obj[-1] = 1.0

        A_ub = np.hstack((A, -np.ones((R, 1), dtype=np.float64)))
        b_ub = np.zeros(R, dtype=np.float64)

        A_eq = np.zeros((1, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
        A_eq[0, :n] = 1.0
        b_eq = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)

        bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n + [(-1.0, None)]

        try:
            res = linprog(
                c_obj,
                A_ub=A_ub,
                b_ub=b_ub,
                A_eq=A_eq,
                b_eq=b_eq,
                bounds=bounds,
                method="highs",
                options={"presolve": True, "max_iters": 50000},
            )
        except Exception:
            break

        if res is None or res.status != 0 or res.x is None:
            break

        gamma_scaled = float(res.x[-1])
        g = np.asarray(res.x[:n], dtype=np.float64)
        g = np.clip(g, 0.0, None)
        S = float(np.sum(g))
        if S <= 1e-15:
            break
        g = g / S

        gamma_abs = gamma_scaled * M
        if gamma_abs < best_gamma_abs:
            best_gamma_abs = gamma_abs
            best_g = g.copy()

        # Check all constraints; add the worst violation if there is one.
        try:
            L = 2.0 * fft_cross_conv(a, g) - c
        except Exception:
            L = None

        if L is not None and L.size == c.size:
            maxL = float(np.max(L))
            rmax = int(np.argmax(L))
            if maxL > gamma_abs + 1e-7 and len(current_rows) < 2 * n - 1:
                if rmax not in current_rows:
                    current_rows = np.unique(np.concatenate((current_rows, [rmax])))
                else:
                    break
            else:
                break
        else:
            break

    if best_g is not None and best_gamma_abs < M - 1e-9:
        return normalize_nonneg(best_g)
    return None


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# L-BFGS on softmax parameters
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _objective_global(x):
    ctx = _CTX
    if ctx is None:
        return 0.0

    w = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    a = softmax_from_log(w)

    c = fft_conv(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return 1e100
    M = float(np.max(c))
    T = float(ctx.get("T", 1e-8))
    score = 2.0 * a.size * M

    best_arr = ctx.get("best_score")
    if best_arr is not None and np.isfinite(score) and score < best_arr[0] - 1e-12:
        best_arr[0] = float(score)
        ctx["best_w"] = w.copy()
        st = ctx.get("state")
        if st is not None:
            update_global(st, a, float(score))

    if T <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(T):
        return float(M)

    s = c - M
    ex = np.exp(s / T)
    sumexp = float(np.sum(ex))
    if not np.isfinite(sumexp) or sumexp <= 0.0:
        return float(M)

    val = 0.5 * M + 0.5 * T * float(np.log(sumexp))
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        return float(M)
    return val


def _gradient_global(x):
    ctx = _CTX
    if ctx is None:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)

    w = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    a = softmax_from_log(w)

    c = fft_conv(a)
    M = float(np.max(c))
    T = float(ctx.get("T", 1e-8))

    if T <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(T):
        p = np.zeros_like(c)
        p[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        s = c - M
        p = np.exp(s / T)
        ps = float(np.sum(p))
        if not np.isfinite(ps) or ps <= 0.0:
            p = np.zeros_like(c)
            p[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        else:
            p /= ps

    r = cross_pow(p, a)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(r)):
        r = np.where(np.isfinite(r), r, 0.0)

    grad = a * (r - float(np.dot(a, r)))
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
    return grad


def _minimize_callback(xk):
    return time.monotonic() > _STOP_DEADLINE


def run_lbfbs(a, time_limit, state, rng):
    """
    L-BFGS-B with a cooling temperature schedule.
    Uses a softmax parameterization and the smooth objective
        (T/2) logsumexp(c/T),
    whose gradient in log-mass coordinates is consistent.
    """
    if minimize is None:
        return normalize_nonneg(a)

    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    n = a.size
    if n < 2 or n > 2200:
        return a

    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(time_limit))

    if state is not None and state.get("seq") is not None:
        sc_state = float(state.get("score", float("inf")))
        sc_a = score_positive(a)
        if sc_state < sc_a - 1e-12:
            a = normalize_nonneg(state["seq"])

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = score_positive(a)
    if state is not None:
        update_global(state, best_a, best_score)

    fracs = [0.45, 0.25, 0.13, 0.07, 0.035, 0.018, 0.010, 0.005, 0.0025]
    frac_idx = 0
    no_improve = 0

    while time.monotonic() + 30.0 < deadline:
        frac = fracs[frac_idx % len(fracs)]
        if no_improve > 2 and frac < 0.12:
            frac = 0.25

        M = max(1e-15, best_score / (2.0 * n))
        T = max(1e-12, frac * M)

        ctx = {
            "T": float(T),
            "state": state,
            "best_score": [best_score],
            "best_w": None,
        }
        global _CTX
        _CTX = ctx

        w0 = np.log(best_a + 1e-15)
        w0 -= float(np.mean(w0))

        maxiter = 80 if n > 1024 else 140
        bounds = [(-28.0, 28.0)] * n
        global _STOP_DEADLINE
        _STOP_DEADLINE = max(time.monotonic(), deadline - 10.0)

        try:
            res = minimize(
                _objective_global,
                w0,
                jac=_gradient_global,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                bounds=bounds,
                callback=_minimize_callback,
                options={
                    "maxiter": maxiter,
                    "ftol": 1e-11,
                    "gtol": 1e-6,
                    "maxls": 18,
                    "disp": False,
                },
            )
        except Exception:
            res = None

        if ctx.get("best_w") is not None:
            w_best = ctx["best_w"]
        elif res is not None and res.x is not None:
            w_best = res.x
        else:
            w_best = w0

        a_new = softmax_from_log(w_best)
        sc_new = score_positive(a_new)

        if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < best_score - 1e-11:
            best_a = a_new
            best_score = sc_new
            no_improve = 0
            frac_idx += 1
            if state is not None:
                update_global(state, best_a, best_score)
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if state is not None and state.get("seq") is not None:
                sc_state = float(state.get("score", float("inf")))
                if sc_state < best_score - 1e-12:
                    best_a = normalize_nonneg(state["seq"])
                    best_score = sc_state

            if no_improve >= 3:
                best_a = random_perturb(best_a, rng, 0.18)
                best_score = score_positive(best_a)
                no_improve = 0
                frac_idx = 0
                if state is not None:
                    update_global(state, best_a, best_score)
            else:
                frac_idx += 1

    # Tighten locally by exact score.
    scx = score_exact_limit(best_a)
    if np.isfinite(scx) and scx < best_score:
        best_a = normalize_nonneg(best_a)
        best_score = scx
        if state is not None:
            update_global(state, best_a, best_score)

    return best_a


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# General local refinement loop
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def local_loop(a, time_limit, state, rng):
    """
    Gradient / LP / random local refinement without quasi-Newton.
    """
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    n = a.size
    if n < 2:
        return a

    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(time_limit))

    if state is not None and state.get("seq") is not None:
        sc_state = float(state.get("score", float("inf")))
        sc_a = score_positive(a)
        if sc_state < sc_a - 1e-12:
            a = normalize_nonneg(state["seq"])

    c = fft_conv(a)
    M = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    best_score = float(2.0 * n * M)
    best_a = a.copy()
    if state is not None:
        update_global(state, best_a, best_score)

    no_improve = 0
    temp_frac = 0.16
    it = 0
    last_lp_time = time.monotonic()
    lp_time_spent = 0.0
    max_lp_time = max(30.0, 0.30 * float(time_limit))
    use_lp = linprog is not None and n <= 768

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        c = fft_conv(a)
        M = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
        cur = float(2.0 * n * M)

        if cur < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = cur
            best_a = a.copy()
            no_improve = 0
            temp_frac = max(0.02, temp_frac * 0.96)
            if state is not None:
                update_global(state, best_a, best_score)
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if no_improve > 45:
            temp_frac = min(0.80, temp_frac * 1.35)
            a = random_perturb(a, rng, 0.25)
            continue

        moved = False
        now = time.monotonic()

        if use_lp and (it % 6 == 0) and (now - last_lp_time > 1.5) and (lp_time_spent < max_lp_time):
            t0 = time.monotonic()
            rows = choose_active_rows(c, min(2 * n - 1, 250))
            g = None
            try:
                g = solve_lp_sot(a, M, rows)
            except Exception:
                g = None

            if g is None and n <= 650 and (it % 18 == 0):
                try:
                    g = solve_lp_tangent(a, c, M, rows=rows, max_rows=min(2 * n - 1, 250))
                except Exception:
                    g = None
            lp_time_spent += max(0.0, time.monotonic() - t0)
            last_lp_time = time.monotonic()

            if g is not None:
                h, sc = line_search_mix(a, g, cur)
                if sc < cur - 1e-12:
                    a = h
                    moved = True
                    no_improve = 0

        if not moved and time.monotonic() < deadline:
            h = smooth_step(a, c, M, temp_frac, cur)
            if h is not None:
                a = h
                moved = True
                no_improve = 0

        if not moved:
            a = random_perturb(a, rng, 0.07)

    scx = score_exact_limit(best_a)
    if np.isfinite(scx) and scx < best_score:
        best_a = normalize_nonneg(best_a)
        best_score = scx
        if state is not None:
            update_global(state, best_a, best_score)

    return best_a


def special_refine(a, time_limit, state, rng):
    """Use L-BFGS first, then the general loop, on a candidate."""
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    if state is not None and state.get("seq") is not None:
        sc_state = float(state.get("score", float("inf")))
        sc_a = score_positive(a)
        if sc_state < sc_a - 1e-12:
            a = normalize_nonneg(state["seq"])

    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(time_limit))
    remaining = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    if remaining <= 0:
        return a

    if minimize is not None and a.size <= 1600 and remaining > 60:
        lbfbs_time = min(0.55 * remaining, max(40.0, 320.0 if a.size <= 640 else 180.0))
        if lbfbs_time > 35.0:
            a = run_lbfbs(a, lbfbs_time, state, rng)
            if state is not None and state.get("seq") is not None:
                sc_state = float(state.get("score", float("inf")))
                sc_a = score_positive(a)
                if sc_state < sc_a - 1e-12:
                    a = normalize_nonneg(state["seq"])

    remaining = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    if remaining > 30.0:
        a = local_loop(a, remaining, state, rng)

    return a


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Main entry point
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800):
    """
    Search for the best nonnegative height sequence.

    Returns a Python list of floats.
    """
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) if seed is not None else 42)
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 1800.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget) or budget <= 0:
        budget = 1800.0

    hard_deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, budget - 70.0)
    state = {"score": float("inf"), "seq": None, "top": []}

    # Load the previously found construction, if available.
    provided = load_provided_sequence()
    if provided is not None:
        update_global(state, provided, score_positive(provided))

    # Guaranteed valid fallbacks.
    fallback1 = normalize_nonneg(np.array([1.0 / 3.0, 2.0 / 3.0], dtype=np.float64))
    update_global(state, fallback1, score_positive(fallback1))
    fallback2 = normalize_nonneg(np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64))
    update_global(state, fallback2, score_positive(fallback2))

    # Quick inexpensive starts so later phases have a decent best.
    if time.monotonic() + 8.0 < hard_deadline:
        add_two_block_candidates(512, state)
        add_random_block_candidates(512, state, rng, 40)
    if time.monotonic() + 8.0 < hard_deadline:
        add_two_block_candidates(1024, state)
        add_random_block_candidates(1024, state, rng, 25)

    if state.get("seq") is None:
        primary = make_initial(512, 5, rng)
    elif provided is not None and provided.size >= 256:
        primary = normalize_nonneg(provided)
    else:
        primary = normalize_nonnorm if False else normalize_nonneg(state["seq"])

    tasks = []

    # Refine the original provided length when useful and distinct.
    if provided is not None and 256 <= provided.size <= 2200 and provided.size != 512:
        tasks.append((normalize_nonneg(provided), 330.0))

    # Main resolutions.
    tasks.append((resize_to_length(primary, 512), 480.0))
    tasks.append((resize_to_length(primary, 1024), 400.0))
    tasks.append((resize_to_length(primary, 2048), 220.0))

    # A few brief diverse starts around 512.
    diverse_kinds = [5, 4, 0, 7, 1]
    for k in diverse_kinds:
        if time.monotonic() + 25.0 > hard_deadline:
            break
        tasks.append((make_initial(512, k, rng, state.get("seq"), provided), 120.0))

    for seq, dur in tasks:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now + 30.0 > hard_deadline:
            break
        limit = min(float(dur), hard_deadline - now)
        if limit < 25.0:
            continue

        # If a very bad large candidate is present and we already have a strong
        # smaller result, spend less time on it.
        if limit > 300.0 and np.isfinite(state.get("score", float("inf"))):
            sc = score_positive(seq)
            if sc > max(2.0, 1.4 * state["score"]) and seq.size > 512:
                limit = min(limit, 120.0)

        try:
            special_refine(seq, limit, state, rng)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Final selection: prefer official evaluation when available.
    best_seq = None
    best_val = float("inf")

    if state.get("top") is not None and len(state["top"]) > 0:
        for _, cand in state["top"]:
            cand = normalize_nonneg(cand)
            if cand.size == 0:
                continue
            pyseq = [float(x) for x in cand]
            official = None
            try:
                official = evaluate_sequence(pyseq)
            except Exception:
                official = None

            if official is not None and np.isfinite(official):
                val = float(official)
            else:
                val = state.get("score", float("inf"))

            if np.isfinite(val) and val < best_val - 1e-12:
                best_val = val
                best_seq = pyseq

    if best_seq is None:
        if state.get("seq") is not None:
            best_seq = [float(x) for x in normalize_nonneg(state["seq"])]
        else:
            best_seq = [float(1.0 / 3.0), float(2.0 / 3.0)]

    # Very last safety check.
    if len(best_seq) == 0:
        best_seq = [float(1.0 / 3.0), float(2.0 / 3.0)]

    return best_seq
```
